# FED-13 RPG All Prompts

Every prompt Git-FED needs at this point to create FED-13's assets and animations himself. One prompt per asset or animation. Paste one prompt as-is into an image or animation generator to make that asset or animation. The game is design only, no build yet, assets first.

Style line on every prompt: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor. Every sprite prompt needs six views: front view, back view, left side view, right side view, plus battle front view and battle back view, in one prompt per subject. Every sprite prompt spells out all six views in the prompt body and in a Required views sentence right before the Style sentence. Never write left and right shorthand. Every sprite prompt also carries a Battle sprites sentence right after the Required views sentence: the battle front view and battle back view are the Pokemon style 1v1 battle sprites, side view, larger scale, the subject facing its opponent, and the front view, back view, left side view, and right side view are the FFTA grid battle unit sprites, smaller scale, isometric grid units.

No prompt describes a weapon, item, or Mankind from any other game. All weapons and items are FED-13 originals, no Keyblades, no weapons from other games. The Soulblade is an original FED-13 straight great blade with a wide straight crossguard and a bright crystal core, pale silver metal and deep blue crystal, leather wrapped handle, engraved lines. It is not key shaped, has no toothed tip, no bow guard, and is never held like a key.

Total: 928 prompts.

## Sprite Types

Every sprite type the game needs, with its count and whether prompts exist for it. Base Feral prompts in Section 2 and base Bondkin prompts in Sections 6 and 13 are treated as Adult Growth Stage at 0.85 size. Child is 0.7 size and Elder is 1.0 size. Rebuilt 2026-11-23: Section 2 is now 134 Feral prompts matching the renewed Codex, Sections 3 and 4 are now 134 Hollow and 134 Echo prompts matching the renewed variant files, Section 1 now includes the 42 EX Arts and Lost Arts effect prompts, Section 13 now includes the 5 core Bondkin Adult base prompts as 13f, and Section 14 adds the 9 Totem Forms for the other bloodlines. Rebuilt again 2026-11-23: Sections 15 to 18 add town art, Mankind class sprites, Apex Form sprites, and named vehicle art, prompt 656 is now the first named vehicle, and the unnamed vehicles placeholder is gone. Battle system: every sprite prompt carries the Battle sprites sentence, battle front view and battle back view are the Pokemon style 1v1 battle sprites, and front view, back view, left side view, and right side view are the FFTA grid battle unit sprites.

## Section 1: Effect Animation Prompts (171)

## 1. Element attack effects (12)

1. **Fire attack.** Flames erupt from the ground in a rising spiral around the target, orange and red tongues of fire licking upward while embers and black smoke drift off, the screen flashing hot at the moment of impact as the camera pushes in fast. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
2. **Water attack.** A heavy surge of blue water crashes over the target in a wide arc, droplets and spray bursting outward while the ground flashes wet and reflective, the camera tracking the wave from the side as it breaks. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
3. **Electric attack.** Jagged yellow-white lightning forks down from above in three quick strikes, the air crackling with small static sparks and the target flashing white at each hit, the camera shaking slightly on every strike. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
4. **Earth attack.** Stone spikes burst up from the ground under the target in a staggered row, brown and grey rock shards flying while dust clouds roll outward, the ground shaking as the last spike slams home. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
5. **Wind attack.** A sharp green-white gust spirals around the target, leaves and dust caught in the vortex while the air visibly bends, the camera circling slowly as the wind tightens then releases in a burst. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
6. **Ice attack.** A blizzard burst of pale blue ice shards launches at the target in a swirling storm, the shards spinning and striking in a tight spiral while snow explodes outward on impact, the camera spinning with the storm. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
7. **Dark attack.** Black and violet shadow smoke coils up from cracks in the ground, wrapping the target in a dark haze while purple points of light flicker inside the smoke, the screen dimming at the edges as the camera tilts slightly and the shadow bursts outward. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
8. **Ghost attack.** A pale translucent wisp drifts through the target, silver and white, leaving a fading trail while ghostly hands reach briefly then dissolve, the scene going soft and misty as the camera drifts slowly through the fog. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
9. **Time attack.** The scene stutters and rewinds, clock hands and golden sand particles spinning backward around the target while everything freezes for a beat then snaps forward, the camera zooming in on a single frozen moment. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
10. **Space attack.** Golden orbs of light drop from a starry rift above, each one bursting into a small cross of light while the ground glows with a circular glyph, the camera pulling up and back as the rift opens. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
11. **Mirage attack.** Shimmering silver mirrors appear and shatter around the target, each shard reflecting a warped copy of the scene while rainbow glints scatter, the camera cutting between three quick angles as the mirrors break. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
12. **Physical slash and strike.** A bright white slash arc cuts across the target in a single fast diagonal, a thin afterimage trailing the blade while a small shockwave ring and dust burst mark the impact point, the camera snapping in tight on the cut. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

## 2. Crafts (30)

13. **Rush (Hybrid).** The Bondkin dashes forward in a low blur, shoulder first, a short white speed line trailing behind while the target is shoved backward a step with a dust burst at its feet, the camera tracking the dash in one smooth pan. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
14. **Wild Swing (Hybrid).** The Bondkin spins once with its blade held wide, a full circular arc of light sweeping around it while dust and grass kick up in a ring, the camera pulling back to show the whole circle. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
15. **First Aid (Hybrid).** Soft green light rises from the Bondkin's hands and settles over the ally in slow waves, small glowing crosses drifting upward while the ally's outline brightens, the camera holding steady on the gentle glow. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
16. **Throw (Hybrid).** The Bondkin hurls its blade in a straight spinning throw, the weapon flashing as it spins end over end while a sharp impact burst and small shockwave mark the landing, the blade returning in a quick arc as the camera follows the throw. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
17. **Ultima Shot, S-Craft (Hybrid).** A single blazing arrow of white-gold light streaks across the battlefield in a dead straight line, leaving a burning trail while the impact blooms into a wide ring of light, the camera zooming fast along the arrow's path. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
18. **Saint Cross, S-Craft (Hybrid).** A huge cross of holy golden light slams down onto the target from above, four beams meeting at the center while white feathers and light particles scatter, the ground glowing in a cross pattern as the camera looks down from a high angle. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
19. **Mog Attack (Puffkin).** The Puffkin leaps up and slams down with its whole body, a small round shockwave and a puff of dust marking the landing while its pom bounces with a bright flash, the camera bouncing slightly with the impact. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
20. **Fireshot (Puffkin).** The Puffkin fires a single compact fireball from its gun, the shot trailing orange sparks while a sharp pop and a small flame burst mark the hit, smoke curling up after as the camera snaps to the target. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
21. **Cuisine (Puffkin).** The Puffkin waves a small chime and a warm feast glow washes over the ally, steam and golden sparkles rising while the ally is wrapped in a soft amber light that pulses once, the camera drifting in close on the warm glow. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
22. **Gil Toss (Puffkin).** The Puffkin throws a spinning shower of gold coins, the coins glinting as they arc through the air while each coin bursts into a small golden spark on impact, the camera following the glittering arc. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
23. **Ultima Charge, S-Craft (Puffkin).** The Puffkin gathers a huge ball of white-violet energy above its head, the energy crackling and growing while it hurls it down in a blinding column of light, the camera shaking as the column hits. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
24. **Chocobo Rush, S-Craft (Puffkin).** A stampede of golden chocobos thunders across the battlefield in a line, feathers and dust flying while each bird flashes past the target in turn, the camera panning fast alongside the stampede. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
25. **Drain (Elderkin).** Dark red tendrils reach from the Elderkin to the target, siphoning glowing red orbs of life back along the tendrils while the Elderkin brightens as the orbs arrive, the camera holding on the flowing line. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
26. **Water, Craft (Elderkin).** The Elderkin raises its mace and a column of deep blue water erupts upward from beneath the target, the water twisting into a spiral before crashing down while droplets catch the light, the camera tilting up with the column. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
27. **Control (Elderkin).** The Elderkin plays a short melody on its instrument, musical notes in glowing gold drifting toward the target and wrapping around its head like a crown while the target's eyes flash gold, the camera focusing on the notes. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
28. **Flare (Elderkin).** A small sun ignites above the Elderkin, swelling into a blazing white-gold orb then bursting outward in a ring of solar fire, the whole screen washing bright for a beat as the camera pulls back from the glare. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
29. **Ultima Blow, S-Craft (Elderkin).** The Elderkin slams its mace down and a massive pillar of white-violet Ultima energy erupts from the ground, runes circling the pillar while the impact shakes the screen, the camera rising slowly along the pillar. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
30. **Giga Flare, S-Craft (Elderkin).** A colossal flare of white and orange fire expands from the Elderkin in a slow widening dome, the edges crackling with solar sparks while the ground glows molten beneath it, the camera pulling far back to show the full dome. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
31. **Swarmstrike (Harefolk).** The Harefolk lunges forward in a quick low strike, its blade leaving three thin parallel light trails while a small burst of silver sparks marks the hit, the camera snapping forward with the lunge. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
32. **Fire Whip (Harefolk).** The Harefolk cracks a whip of living flame, the whip curling through the air in a bright orange arc while embers fly off its tip, the crack landing with a sharp flame burst as the camera tracks the whip's curve. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
33. **Cure (Harefolk).** Soft pink and white light blooms over the ally like opening petals, gentle sparkles drifting down while the ally glows warm and steady, the camera holding on the soft bloom. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
34. **Doubleshot (Harefolk).** The Harefolk fires two arrows in rapid succession, the first trailing blue light and the second trailing green, both striking a heartbeat apart with twin impact flashes as the camera cuts between the two shots. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
35. **Ultima Masher, S-Craft (Harefolk).** The Harefolk vanishes and reappears in a blur of afterimages around the target, each afterimage striking with a flash of white light while the final strike lands with a huge starburst, the camera spinning to follow the blurs. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
36. **Last Breath, S-Craft (Harefolk).** The Harefolk exhales a pale mist that drifts slowly toward the target, the mist wrapping the target and dimming its light while the scene goes quiet and grey, the camera pushing in slowly as the mist closes. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
37. **Jump (Drakescale).** The Drakescale leaps high into the air, spear raised, hanging for a beat against the sky then diving down in a steep plunge, the impact cracking the ground with a dust ring as the camera follows the arc up and down. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
38. **Fire Breath (Drakescale).** The Drakescale rears back and breathes a wide cone of orange flame, the fire rolling forward in a wave while embers and smoke billow, the camera set low to catch the flames sweeping over it. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
39. **Revive (Drakescale).** Golden light rains down in slow shafts onto the fallen ally, the ally rising as the light gathers while wings of light flare briefly behind it, the camera rising with the ally. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
40. **Body Slam (Drakescale).** The Drakescale charges forward and slams its whole body into the target, a heavy dust explosion and a ring shockwave marking the hit while the target is knocked back a step, the camera shaking hard on impact. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
41. **Ultima Sword, S-Craft (Drakescale).** The Drakescale raises a blade wreathed in white-violet Ultima magic, the blade growing into a giant sword of light then coming down in one massive vertical cut, a towering light pillar rising on impact as the camera tracks the blade down. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
42. **Wyrmkiller, S-Craft (Drakescale).** The Drakescale's spear ignites with dragon-slaying red light, the spear thrown in a blazing spiral with dragon-shaped flame trailing behind it, the impact bursting into red and gold scales of light as the camera spins with the spear. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

## 3. Arts, one per element (7)

43. **Earth Art.** The Bondkin traces a brown earthen casting circle on the ground, stone glyphs rising in a ring while a great stone slab crashes down on the target with a heavy dust plume, the camera holding low on the circle. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
44. **Water Art.** The Bondkin traces a blue water casting circle, the circle filling with rippling light while a tall fountain of water bursts upward and folds over the target, the camera rising with the fountain. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
45. **Fire Art.** The Bondkin traces a red fire casting circle, embers spiraling up from the ring while a phoenix-shaped flame bursts from the circle and dives onto the target, the camera following the flame bird. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
46. **Wind Art.** The Bondkin traces a green wind casting circle, the air inside the ring spinning into a tornado while the tornado lifts leaves and dust as it sweeps the target, the camera circling the vortex. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
47. **Time Art.** The Bondkin traces a gold time casting circle, clockwork rings rotating slowly around it while the scene freezes in a golden stillness before time snaps forward in a flash, the camera zooming on the frozen instant. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
48. **Space Art.** The Bondkin traces a violet space casting circle, stars appearing inside the ring while a golden sphere drops from a rift and bursts into a cross of light, the camera pulling up through the rift. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
49. **Mirage Art.** The Bondkin traces a silver mirage casting circle, the circle shimmering like a heat haze while a phantom fortress of light appears and fires a beam of silver lasers, the camera cutting between the fortress and the beam. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

## 4. Surge Forms (5)

50. **Gale Form transformation.** Wind rushes in from all sides and lifts the Bondkin off the ground, green-white gusts wrapping it in a fast spiral while its outline blurs with speed lines, then the wind bursts outward and the Bondkin lands in Gale Form with a sharp gust as the camera spins once around it. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
51. **Aegis Form transformation.** A heavy golden shield of light forms around the Bondkin, the shield folding in plates like armor while blue runes glow across its surface, the Bondkin stepping forward as the shield settles as the camera pushes in on the glowing plates. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
52. **Zephyr Form transformation.** Soft pale wind lifts the Bondkin gently upward, white feathers and petals drifting around it while its form glows with a calm sky-blue light, the Bondkin floating down in Zephyr Form as the camera drifts up with it. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
53. **Tempest Form transformation.** Storm clouds gather above the Bondkin and lightning crackles down around it, the Bondkin spinning in the air as storm winds whip while its form flashes with electric blue, the camera shaking as thunder cracks and the Bondkin lands in Tempest Form. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
54. **Zenith Form transformation.** A pillar of pure white-gold light falls from the sky onto the Bondkin, the Bondkin rising up inside the pillar while its form shines with starlight, the pillar fading to reveal Zenith Form as the camera pulls back from the blinding light. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

## 5. Totem Spirit moves (5)

55. **Dawnwarden, Hybrid Totem Spirit.** A towering guardian of dawn light rises behind the Bondkin, its shield of sunrise gold spreading wide while the guardian slams its shield down in a wave of morning light, the camera pulling back to show the guardian's full height. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
56. **Brightpom Sovereign, Puffkin Totem Spirit.** A giant radiant Puffkin spirit appears in a burst of pink and gold pom light, its crown glowing while it claps its paws and a shower of golden light rains onto the target, the camera tilting up to the spirit. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
57. **Quiet Flame Sage, Elderkin Totem Spirit.** A robed sage of quiet blue flame materializes in a slow swirl of embers, raising a staff of still fire while a single precise beam of blue flame strikes the target, the camera holding on the sage's calm glow. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
58. **Moonhare Matriarch, Harefolk Totem Spirit.** A great silver moonhare spirit leaps down from a full moon, moonlight trailing behind it while its ears glow, it kicks forward in a burst of silver light as the camera follows the leap. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
59. **Emberwyrm King, Drakescale Totem Spirit.** A massive ember wyrm coils out of the ground in a ring of molten fire, its scales glowing red-gold while it breathes a huge dragon fire blast across the target, the camera pulling back from the flames. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

## 6. Battle system effects (16)

60. **Link Attack.** Two allies flash in turn, a bright line of light connecting them while they strike the target together in one synchronized hit, a double impact burst and a shared flash marking the blow as the camera cuts between the two allies. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
61. **Break.** The target's guard shatters in a burst of cracked glass-like shards, the shards flying outward in slow motion while the target staggers as its defense breaks, the camera zooming in on the shattering guard. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
62. **Unbalanced.** The target wobbles and tilts, wavy distortion lines circling its head while small stars spin around it, the camera tilting sideways to match the wobble. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
63. **S-Break.** The screen darkens and the Bondkin flashes with a full CP gauge burning gold, time seeming to stop while the S-Craft strikes in a single overwhelming burst of light, the camera zooming hard on the impact. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
64. **Burn status.** Orange flames lick up around the target in small bursts, embers rising while the target flashes red with each flare, the camera holding on the flickering fire. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
65. **Freeze status.** Pale blue frost creeps over the target from the ground up, ice crystals forming on its outline while the target locks in a frozen pose with a cold blue glow, the camera holding still on the ice. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
66. **Sleep status.** Soft purple Z letters and dream bubbles drift up from the target, its outline going limp and dim while a gentle pulsing glow rises and falls like slow breathing, the camera drifting slowly downward. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
67. **Poison status.** Sickly green bubbles rise from the target, a purple-green haze clinging to it while the target flashes dull green with each pulse, the camera holding on the toxic haze. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
68. **Paralysis status.** Yellow electric sparks jitter across the target in short bursts, its body twitching and freezing mid-motion while static crackles in the air, the camera shaking slightly with each spark. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
69. **Silence status.** A grey seal ring closes over the target, sound waves visibly dying around it while a dull grey tint washes over the target, the camera focusing on the sealing ring. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
70. **Confuse status.** Swirling pink and purple spirals spin around the target's head, its outline wavering and doubling while question marks pop and fade, the camera swaying gently side to side. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
71. **Healing.** Warm green and white light flows over the ally in a rising wave, small glowing leaves drifting up while the ally brightens steadily, the camera holding on the gentle light. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
72. **Buff.** Golden arrows of light rise from the ally, a bright aura flaring around it while small stars orbit its outline, the camera pushing in on the glowing aura. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
73. **Debuff.** Dark purple chains of light wrap around the target and pull downward, its glow dimming while small downward arrows fall around it, the camera tilting down with the sinking light. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
74. **Critical hit.** The strike lands with a huge white flash and a sharp starburst, the impact ring expanding fast while the screen shakes once hard, the camera snapping in tight on the burst. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
75. **Miss and dodge.** The attacker swings through empty air, the target blurring sideways in a quick afterimage while a small puff of dust marks where it stood, the camera panning fast to the dodged position. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

## 7. Bondkin and Beast effects (8)

76. **Catch a Beast.** The target is pulled toward a glowing capture light, shrinking into a bright orb while the orb flashes three times with a rising chime, then settling with a soft golden glow as the camera zooms in on the orb. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
77. **Beast evolution, Stage 1 to 2.** The Beast glows white and grows taller, its silhouette stretching and reshaping while light bursts outward as its new form settles, the camera pulling back to show the growth. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
78. **Beast evolution, Stage 2 to 3.** The Beast glows gold and grows to its full adult size, its silhouette expanding with a slow powerful light while the ground shakes slightly as it lands in its final form, the camera pulling far back. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
79. **Beast fusion.** Two Beasts glow and drift toward each other, their outlines merging in a swirl of light while a bright flash marks them becoming one new fused Beast, the camera spinning slowly around the merge. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
80. **Egg hatching.** The egg rocks and cracks, light spilling from the cracks while the shell breaks apart in glowing pieces, a small Beast stepping out in a soft flash as the camera zooms in on the hatching. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
81. **Bondkin child birth.** Warm light gathers in the daycare, a soft glow blooming like a sunrise while a small Bondkin child appears in the light with a gentle sparkle, the camera holding on the warm glow. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
82. **Morpher morph.** The Morpher Bondkin holds up a glowing Soul, the Soul's light washing over it while its body reshapes into the Beast type in a swirl of mist, the camera circling as the new form settles. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
83. **Summon.** A magic circle burns bright on the ground, light rising in a column while the summoned ally steps out of the light with a flash, the camera rising with the column. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

84. **Hollow transformation.** Dark smoke pours over the Beast, its colors draining to black while red eyes flare open, a shadow aura rising as the camera dims and the Hollow form settles. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
85. **Echo transformation.** Pale mist wraps the Beast, its colors fading to white and silver while its outline turns translucent and ghostly, a soft glow rising as the camera drifts through the mist. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

87. **Echo region entrance.** Bright pale light washes over the scene as the party enters the Echo region, silver mist drifting up while everything goes soft and luminous, the camera pulling back into the light. 88. **Portal between disconnected worlds.** A swirling ring of light opens in the air, stars and colors spinning inside it while the party steps through as the ring flares, the camera passing through the portal with them. 90. **Sky Galleon.** The great Sky Galleon sails across the sky, its sails billowing with wind while clouds part around its hull, golden light catching the deck as the camera sweeps alongside the ship. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
91. **Golden Strider ride.** The Golden Strider runs with long graceful strides, golden feathers streaming behind it while dust kicks up in its wake, the camera tracking beside the ride. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
92. **World weapon acquisition.** Light gathers around the new weapon as it rises from a glowing pedestal, the weapon spinning slowly in the air while a burst of world-colored light marks the Mankind trainer taking it, the camera circling the weapon. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
93. **Bike ride.** The bike speeds along the path, wheels spinning in a blur while wind lines stream past, the rider leaning into the ride as the camera tracks fast from the side. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
94. **Vehicle tuning and modification.** The vehicle glows as new parts snap into place, gears and bolts of light circling it while sparks fly from each fitted part, the camera circling the vehicle. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
95. **Vehicle repair at Mechanic Station.** The Mechanic Station's tools glow and sparks fly over the damaged vehicle, dents smoothing out in waves of light while the vehicle shines as the repair finishes, the camera holding on the work. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
96. **Vehicle durability breaking.** The vehicle shudders and sparks, cracks of light spreading across it while a puff of smoke rises and a dull clank marks it stopping dead, the camera shaking with the breakdown. ## 10. Systems and places (18)

98. **Puffkin shop.** The Puffkin shopkeeper hops up with a cheerful bounce, its shop sign glowing warm gold while coins and sparkles drift in the air, the camera panning across the shop front. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

101. **Mystical orb guide appearing.** A mystical orb fades in from a swirl of starlight, hovering and pulsing with soft blue light while small sparkles orbit it, the camera holding on the orb. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
102. **Fairy guide appearing.** A tiny fairy flutters in on glowing wings, leaving a trail of golden sparkles while hovering with a bright flash, the camera tracking the fluttering light. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

104. **Level up for Bondkin.** The Bondkin glows with its bloodline color and grows a little taller, light swirling around it in a spiral while a bright flash marks it settling, the camera circling the glow. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

106. **U-Material upgrade.** U-Material shards fly into the weapon, the weapon glowing brighter with each shard while runes ignite along the blade, a final bright flash marking the upgrade complete as the camera pushes in on the blade. 107. **Core Shard swap.** The old Core Shard lifts out of the Lattice with a dimming glow, the new Core Shard sliding in with a bright flare of its element color while the Lattice pulses once, the camera focusing on the swap. 108. **Lattice slot.** A Shard clicks into the Lattice slot, the slot lighting up with the Shard's color while light flows through the Lattice lines, the camera zooming in on the slot. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
109. **Day to night change.** The sky darkens in a slow sweep, stars fading in one by one while the landscape cools to blue, the camera holding wide as the light changes. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
110. **Weather change.** Clouds roll in and rain begins to fall, raindrops catching the light while the ground darkens with wet, the camera tilting up to the changing sky. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
111. **Desert heat.** Heat waves shimmer up from the sand, the air wavering while dust devils spin in the distance, the camera holding on the shimmering horizon. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
112. **Poisonous swamp.** Green bubbles rise from the murky water, toxic mist drifting low while the trees drip with sickly glow, the camera pushing slowly through the mist. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
113. **Stinky area.** Green-brown stink clouds puff up from the ground, wavy stink lines rising while the air goes murky, the camera holding on the rising clouds. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

## 14. EX Arts and Lost Arts effect prompts (42)

130. **Geo Quake (CS2).** The ground splits open in a slow jagged crack under the target, brown stone slabs heaving upward one after another while dust and small rocks tumble down, the earth rumbling as the last slab slams back down with a heavy thud, the camera holding low on the shaking ground. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
131. **Aqua Matter (CS2).** A violent torrent of deep blue water crashes down on the target in a churning wave, white foam and spray bursting outward while the water swirls and pulls back, the ground left shining wet as the camera tracks the wave from the side. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
132. **Lordly Flare (CS2).** A tall column of red and orange flame roars up around the target, the fire twisting like a burning tower while embers spiral upward and the air shimmers with heat, the flames collapsing inward in a bright flash as the camera pulls back from the glare. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
133. **Gale Raid (CS2).** Sharp blades of pale green wind slice across the target in fast crossing arcs, the wind howling as leaves and dust spin in the wake of each cut, the last blade landing with a burst of white air as the camera snaps between the crossing strikes. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
134. **Chivalry Runes (CS2).** Silver rune circles spin open around the target, glowing lines of light forming a noble crest in the air while silver blades of light rain down through the crest, each blade leaving a thin silver trail as the runes shatter into bright shards, the camera looking up through the falling blades. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
135. **Thundering Roar (CS2).** A storm cloud gathers low above the target and a thick bolt of yellow lightning crashes down with a deafening flash, thunder rings rolling outward while smaller sparks crawl across the ground, the target flashing white at the strike as the camera shakes hard. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
136. **Blessed Arrow (CS2).** A single golden arrow of holy light streaks down from above and hovers over the ally, the arrow dissolving into warm golden sparks that drift down and settle over the ally in a soft glow, the ally brightening as the light pulses gently, the camera holding on the warm glow. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
137. **Resounding Beat (CS2).** Soft blue musical notes rise and echo outward in slow ripples, the notes glowing as they drift over the ally while a gentle blue light washes across the ally in waves, small chimes of light sparkling with each echo as the camera drifts slowly with the music. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
138. **Resonant Beat (CS2).** A bright green melody blooms around the ally in a ringing chord, glowing notes circling fast and rising in a spiral while green light pulses outward in steady beats, the ally wrapped in a warm green glow that strengthens with each pulse as the camera circles the spiral. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
139. **Radiant Wings (CS2).** Great wings of white and gold light unfold behind the ally, feathers of light scattering as the wings beat once and lift the ally slightly, the ally glowing with a fierce golden aura while the wing light fades slowly, the camera pulling back to show the full span of the wings. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
140. **Burst Drive (CS2).** Golden clock hands spin fast around the ally in a blur, time seeming to stretch and snap as the ally flashes forward in a streak of gold light, the clock hands shattering into bright sparks while the ally stands ready in a burst of speed, the camera zooming fast along the streak. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
141. **Crescent Shell (CS2).** A pale silver crescent moon rises behind the ally and curves into a shell of moonlight, the shell closing gently around the ally in a smooth silver dome, the surface shimmering with soft lunar light while small stars drift across it, the camera holding on the glowing dome. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
142. **Sonic Burst (CS2).** A sharp gust of wind wraps the ally in a fast spinning vortex, white speed lines whipping around the ally while the vortex tightens and then releases in a sudden rush, the ally flashing forward with a trail of wind as the camera spins with the vortex. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
143. **Wild Rage (CS2).** Red fire erupts from the ally in a wild bursting flare, the ally glowing deep red as crimson sparks fly outward in all directions, the ground cracking with red light beneath the ally while the flames roar upward and then settle into a burning red aura, the camera shaking with the eruption. 144. **Ultimate Barrier (CS2).** A massive wall of clear crystal light rises around the ally in tall panels, the panels locking together with a bright chime while blue runes glow along their edges, the barrier holding steady and gleaming as light reflects off every panel, the camera circling the gleaming wall. 145. **Geo Quake (CS3).** The earth erupts in a ring of tall stone pillars around the target, golden cracks of light racing through the ground between the pillars while the pillars slam down in turn with heavy dust bursts, the camera looking down from above as the ring closes. 146. **Aqua Matter (CS3).** A towering spiral of pale blue water rises from below the target and twists upward like a waterspout, the spiral bursting apart at the top into a rain of glittering droplets while the target is caught in the swirling water, the camera rising with the spiral. 151. **Silver Ray (CS3).** A beam of pure silver light falls straight down onto the target, the beam widening as it strikes while silver mirrors flash into being around the target and reflect the light in every direction, the mirrors shattering into bright silver shards as the beam fades, the camera tilting up along the beam. 153. **Blade Dance (CS3).** The ally is wrapped in a whirl of glowing sword light, blades of red, gold, and blue light spinning around the ally in a fast dance while sparks fly from each blade, the blades rising together into a bright spiral above the ally as the ally glows with fierce light, the camera circling the dancing blades. 155. **Saphir Rain (CS3).** Blue gem light falls over all allies like gentle rain, sapphire droplets glowing as they drift down and settle over each ally in turn, the allies brightening with a calm blue glow while small blue crystals sparkle in the air, the camera pulling back to show the rain falling over the whole party. 159. **Radiant Wings (CS3).** Wings of burning gold and white light blaze open behind the ally, the wings shedding bright feathers of light that swirl around the ally in a rising spiral, the ally rising slightly as the wing light flares to a blinding peak and then settles into a strong golden aura, the camera rising with the spiral of feathers. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
160. **Burst Drive (CS3).** The ally shatters the air around itself in a burst of gold and red light, clockwork rings breaking apart as the ally flashes forward in a blazing streak, the streak splitting into bright afterimages that converge back into the ally, the ally standing in a flare of power as the camera snaps forward with the burst. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
161. **Crescent Shell (CS3).** A great full moon of silver light rises over all allies and pours moonlight down in a wide dome, the dome shimmering with lunar runes while the allies glow beneath it in soft silver, the moonlight rippling gently across the dome as it holds, the camera pulling back to show the whole moonlit dome. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
162. **Chivalry Runes (CS3).** A towering crest of silver and blue runes ignites above the target, the runes burning bright as a rain of silver blades falls through them in a straight piercing line, each blade leaving a trail of blue light while the crest cracks and falls apart in glowing shards, the camera looking up through the falling blades. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
163. **Sonic Burst (CS3).** A howling wind barrier forms around the ally in a tight spinning shell, the shell flashing with white and blue light as it spins faster and faster, the ally bursting out of the shell in a streak of wind while the shell dissolves into a rush of air, the camera spinning fast with the shell. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
164. **Golden Howl (CS3).** A great golden howl of light erupts from the ally in a rising wave, the wave rolling outward in rings of gold while the ally glows with a fierce amber aura, sparks of gold flying upward like embers as the wave crashes forward and fades into a steady golden glow, the camera shaking with the rolling wave. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
165. **Ultimate Barrier (CS3).** A perfect dome of white and gold light seals around the ally, the dome glowing with layered rings of light while a soft chime sounds and golden runes drift across its surface, the dome shining like a small sun as it holds firm, the camera circling the bright dome. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
166. **Thundering Roar (CS3).** A colossal storm of lightning crashes down on the target in a blinding white and blue blast, the lightning branching into a web of crackling bolts that strike again and again, the ground glowing electric blue beneath the storm while thunder rolls and the air crackles, the camera pulling far back from the storm. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
167. **Sol Eruption.** A blazing sun rises over the whole battlefield and burns with white gold fire, the sun bursting apart into a rain of searing solar flames that fall on every foe, the flames washing the scene in bright orange light while embers and heat waves rise, the camera pulling back as the sun explodes. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
168. **Artemis' Tears.** A pale full moon rises and weeps tears of silver blue light, the tears falling gently over all allies in a soft glowing rain, each ally brightening as the moonlight settles over them in a calm silver glow, the scene going quiet and luminous while the moon shines above, the camera drifting slowly upward toward the moon. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
169. **Grail Burst.** A golden grail of light appears and overflows with bright starlight, the starlight bursting outward in a wave that sweeps over all allies while time seems to bend and shimmer around them, the allies flashing with gold light as the grail fades into a shower of stars, the camera spinning slowly through the starlight. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
170. **Tempestuous Roar.** A great roar of storm and earth shakes the battlefield, green and gold light rising from the ground in a rolling wave while lightning crackles through the air, all allies glowing with a fierce surging light as the roar echoes and fades into a steady pulsing glow, the camera shaking with the rolling wave. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
171. **Lost Eden.** A holy light descends from a bright rift in the sky and blooms into a garden of white and gold radiance, the light washing over every foe in a blinding wave while pale petals and golden sparks drift through the air, the scene shimmering with sacred light as the rift closes slowly, the camera pulling up through the falling petals. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
