## Section 7: World and Place Art (29)

587. Saltquay, a bright harbor town built around a stone quay, with fishing boats, net drying racks, salt stalls, and gabled houses facing the sea. Gulls circle over the market square where crates of catch, rope, and sailcloth fill the lanes, and lanterns hang from every awning. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
588. Cragfall, a steep mountain town cut into gray stone terraces, with a mine entrance, a smoking forge, cable carts on wooden rails, and slate roofed houses stacked above a cobbled yard. Lantern light glows from workshop windows, and piles of ore, timber, and tools line the narrow streets. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
589. Barleyford, a quiet farming town ringed by golden fields, orchards, and a slow river mill, with timber houses, hay barns, vegetable gardens, and a small green where carts and market baskets gather. Smoke rises from chimneys, laundry lines cross the lanes, and a stone well stands at the center of the square. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
590. Dunewell, a sun baked oasis town of sandstone walls, flat roofs, palm shade, and a deep central well, with cloth awnings, spice stalls, water jars, and a shaded courtyard where travelers rest. Pale dust coats the streets, and carved screens and painted tiles cool the doorways against the bright heat. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
591. Reedbridge, a lakeside canal town of stilt houses, arched bridges, reed boats, and turning water wheels, with flower boxes on every balcony and lanterns reflected in the slow green water. Wooden walkways link the houses, and fishermen mend nets beside moored boats under willow trees. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
592. A haunted house standing alone on a misty hill, a tall dark mansion with broken gates, cracked windows, crooked chimneys, and a tangled garden of dead trees and iron fences. Cold blue light glows in the upper windows, cobwebs hang in the doorways, and pale mist drifts across the overgrown path to the front steps. The world weapon for this world is the wrought iron mace with a caged lantern. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
593. A period animal city with wide cobblestone streets, tall timber and brick buildings, a constable station, shops, cafes, and market stalls sized for many kinds of animals. Painted wooden signs and a detective office front the busy square, and horse drawn carts and crowds move through tree lined avenues. The world weapon for this world is the brass baton with a paw print. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
594. An ocean island of white sand, palm groves, and clear blue water, with voyaging canoes pulled up on the beach, carved wayfinding stones, thatched huts, and fishing nets drying in the sun. Waves break on a coral reef offshore, and ocean magic glimmers in tide pools and carved wooden markers along the shore. The world weapon for this world is the carved wooden paddle axe with wave patterns. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
595. An underwater kingdom of coral towers, shell domes, kelp gardens, and glowing pearl light, with grand halls carved into the reef and schools of bright fish drifting through arched windows. Soft blue water fills every street, and banners of woven seaweed sway above plazas paved with smooth stone and sand. The world weapon for this world is the coral and pearl trident. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
596. A lion kingdom on a wide golden savanna, with tall grass, flat topped trees, a rocky outcrop overlooking the plains, and dens carved into warm stone. Herds graze in the distance, dust rises on the wind, and a royal clearing of packed earth and carved posts sits beneath the open sky. The world weapon for this world is the golden spear with a sun disc pommel. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
597. A hidden tech kingdom tucked behind waterfalls and stone temples, where glowing panels, water channels, and smooth metal bridges blend with carved stone, moss, and gardens. Bright light runs along hidden conduits, and quiet courtyards hold fountains, archives, and workshops behind vine covered walls. The world weapon for this world is the steel sabre with a glowing circuit lined edge. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
598. A fairy tale kingdom in a deep green forest, with mushroom cottages, winding cobbled lanes, a tall castle with blue spires, and apple orchards behind white fences. Flowering vines climb the walls, storybook signs mark the paths, and lanterns glow in windows beneath the trees. The world weapon for this world is the enchanted longbow of pale wood wrapped in flowering vines. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
599. A mind and emotions world inside a vast inner landscape, with bright colored districts for different feelings, floating memory orbs, winding thought paths, and a central headquarters building of glass and light. Soft clouds drift over bridges between emotion zones, and glowing doors open onto quiet rooms filled with memories. The world weapon for this world is the scepter topped with a round orb that shifts through warm and cool colors. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
600. A magic multiverse of floating islands linked by glowing portals, with wizard towers, spell light, starlit bridges, and realms of fire, water, wind, and earth drifting side by side. Ancient runes circle each portal, and robed towers rise from mist above a sea of clouds. The world weapon for this world is the spellblade with a swirling portal ring set into the hilt. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
601. A world of secrecy for The Thirteen, a shadowed city of hidden bases, coded signs, locked doors, and quiet alleys where spies pass messages in plain sight. Plain shop fronts hide stairways and meeting rooms, and lanterns burn low behind shuttered windows. The world weapon for this world is the slim black dagger with a coded rune etched blade. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
602. The dark corridors of The Thirteen, a shifting castle interior of long black halls, white rooms, secret doors, and stairways that lead to new places each time they are walked. Pale light spills from doorways that were not there before, and mirrors, portraits, and locked gates line the silent passages. The world weapon for this world is the pale white halberd with a crescent blade. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
603. The headquarters of The Thirteen, a dark fortress of tall towers, black stone walls, and a grand meeting hall with a long table, high banners, and a ring of thrones. Torchlight flickers over maps, sealed doors, and guarded corridors, and the fortress stands apart on a crag above a cold valley. The world weapon for this world is the dark ceremonial war hammer with a heavy chained head. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
604. A dark Hollow region of blackened trees, shadowed ravines, and thick purple mist, where corrupted light flickers and the ground is cracked and cold. Hollow variants are found in this dark region, and twisted roots and broken stone mark the paths through the gloom. This is the Duskhollow region. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
605. A light Echo region of pale meadows, white trees, and soft glowing mist, where faded colors, ghostly light, and quiet streams give the whole place a distant dreamlike glow. Echo variants are found in this light region, and pale flowers and silver leaves shimmer along the winding paths. This is the Lumenvale region. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
606. A small hometown of familiar streets, home gardens, a training yard, and low houses with tiled roofs around a quiet central square. Neighbors greet each other beside the well, children play near the fences, and the roads out of town lead toward fields and forest. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
607. A vast desert of rolling dunes, cracked earth, bleached bones of old ruins, and a harsh white sun over shimmering heat. Sparse dry shrubs and lone rock spires break the sand, and a distant mirage glimmers above the horizon. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
608. A stinky area of sulfurous bogs, bubbling mud pools, rotten vegetation, and a low yellow haze that hangs over stagnant water. Cracked boards cross the reeking marsh, and flies drift above green scum and half sunken barrels. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
609. A poisonous swamp of toxic green water, venomous thorn vines, glowing mushrooms, and black trees draped in hanging moss. Bubbles rise from the murky surface, and warning signs and broken fences mark the edge of the poisoned reeds. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
610. A dense city of tall buildings, wide plazas, crowded markets, shops, inns, and guild halls, with banners, fountains, and cobbled streets full of carts and people. Smoke rises from many chimneys, and the skyline of towers and rooftops stretches in every direction. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
611. A deep wild forest of towering trees, mossy trails, ferns, and dappled green light, with fallen logs, mushroom rings, and a stream winding between roots. Birds call from the canopy, and the undergrowth grows thick and quiet away from the paths. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
612. A high mountain landscape of sharp peaks, snowfields, rocky trails, pine slopes, and sheer cliffs above deep valleys. Cold wind blows across the ridges, and a narrow pass winds past stone cairns toward a distant summit. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
613. A rugged coast of sea cliffs, tide pools, sandy coves, and crashing waves, with gulls above the rocks and sea spray drifting over the shore. Smooth stones, driftwood, and shells line the beach, and a lighthouse point juts into the open sea. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
614. Ancient ruins of broken columns, fallen walls, cracked statues, and moss covered stone courts half buried in vines and earth. A toppled arch frames a weed choked courtyard, and faded carvings still mark the weathered blocks. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
615. Dark dungeons of stone corridors, torchlit chambers, iron gates, and treasure chests, with traps, locked doors, and stairways descending into deeper halls. Water drips from the ceilings, and carved pillars and old banners line the passages to the lowest rooms. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

## Section 8: Weapons (16)

616. The Soulblade is an original FED-13 weapon, a long straight great blade made for the main player, and it is not a weapon from any other game. Its pale silver metal blade is wide and straight with a wide straight crossguard, a bright crystal core of deep blue crystal set down the center, a leather wrapped handle, and fine engraved lines along the flat of the blade. It is a plain straight sword built for battle, with no extra shapes or ornaments on its tip or guard. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
617. The Gravebell Flail, the haunted house world weapon, a wrought iron flail with a chained head topped by a caged lantern that burns with a pale ghostly flame and a small grave bell that rings as it swings. Dark iron bands, a carved wooden grip, and faint glowing runes give it a spooky mansion feel. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
618. The Pawprint Hammer, the animal city world weapon, a polished brass hammer with a sturdy leather grip and a large engraved paw print on its head. Its clean period lines and city badge emblem suit a period city where animals live and work as officers, detectives, and citizens. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
619. The Wavecrest Cutlass, the ocean island world weapon, a curved cutlass with wave patterns cut into the blade and pale shell inlay along the handle. Its warm wood grip, ocean blue paint, and rope wrapped hilt suit a tropical island of voyaging canoes and wayfinding. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
620. The Tidecaller Trident, the underwater kingdom world weapon, a coral and pearl trident with three curved prongs and a shaft of deep blue metal. Pale coral branches, shining pearl inlay, and flowing seaweed ribbons give it the look of a royal weapon from beneath the sea. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
621. The Sunmane Spear, the lion kingdom savanna world weapon, a long golden spear with a sun disc pommel and a tawny tassel tied below the head. Its warm gold metal, carved wooden shaft, and sun etched blade suit a golden savanna kingdom of lions. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
622. The Geartooth Axe, the hidden tech kingdom world weapon, a steel axe with a gear toothed edge and a grip of dark metal and pale blue light. Its smooth period shape and hidden water powered machinery details suit a secret kingdom of advanced technology. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
623. The Thornrose Rapier, the fairy tale kingdom world weapon, a slender rapier of pale steel wrapped in flowering vines with a thorned guard and a rose shaped pommel. Its storybook carvings, soft green leaves, and small blooming flowers suit an enchanted forest kingdom of castles and old magic. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
624. The Empath Bow, the mind and emotions world weapon, a slender bow of pale silver wood with a string of soft glowing light that shifts through warm and cool colors like changing feelings. Its gentle swirling light and smooth curved limbs suit a strange inner world inside a person's mind. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
625. The Spellweave Staff, the magic multiverse world weapon, a wizard's staff topped with a swirling portal ring and a shaft etched with tiny stars. Its deep purple wood, bright portal glow, and drifting sparks suit a mystical world of portals, wizards, spells, and magical realms. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
626. The Whisperfang, the Thirteen's secrecy world weapon, a slim black dagger with a fang shaped blade etched with coded runes and a dark cloth wrapped grip. Its quiet matte black metal, hidden markings, and plain sheath suit a secret organization of spies and hidden bases. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
627. The Doorwarden Glaive, the Thirteen's dark corridors world weapon, a pale white glaive with a crescent blade and a shaft of shifting mirror metal that reflects a changing corridor. Its clean silver edges, pale white finish, and faint door shaped etchings suit a castle of dark corridors, white rooms, and secret doors. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
628. The Vexbrand, the Thirteen headquarters world weapon, a dark ceremonial longsword with a heavy chained guard and a silver emblem set into its blade. Its dark metal, silver trim, and formal carved handle suit the secret headquarters of a hidden organization. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
629. The Sword opening choice weapon, a straight single edged long sword made as an original FED-13 weapon. Its clean pale steel blade, simple straight guard, and dark leather wrapped handle give it a plain and practical look for an attacking fighter. It belongs to the opening choice only and is not a weapon from any other game. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
630. The Shield opening choice weapon, a round medium shield made as an original FED-13 weapon. Its sturdy wooden face is banded with pale steel, a round metal boss sits at the center, and a leather strapped grip sits on the back. It belongs to the opening choice only and is not a weapon from any other game. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
631. The Staff opening choice weapon, a tall straight staff topped with a crystal orb made as an original FED-13 weapon. Its smooth dark wood shaft, carved rings near the top, and bright glowing crystal orb give it a plain and magical look for a spell caster. It belongs to the opening choice only and is not a weapon from any other game. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

## Section 9: Vehicles (25)

632. The riding horse is a strong bay horse with a glossy chestnut coat, a red and black saddle blanket, a leather saddle with brass fittings, and sturdy shod hooves built for rough roads. Its dark mane and tail flow as it runs, its bridle shines with brass, and it gallops hard across open ground with long smooth strides. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
633. The Cobalt Blue Wave is a smooth four seat carriage in deep cobalt blue with a long curved body, a wide front bench, brass trim, and four sturdy spoked wheels. Its windows are tinted pale blue, its doors open wide for a full team, and it rolls steadily along roads and through towns. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
634. The Metal Gray Wilderness Traveler is a boxy off road trail cart in metal gray with a high roof rack, a front guard bar, a rear spare wheel, and large sturdy wheels for mud and rock. Its square windows and raised frame give it a rugged expedition look, and it climbs rough trails and crosses wild country without slowing down. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
635. The Blue 808 is a compact blue horse buggy with a rounded body, a short rear, round brass lamps, a simple black leather seat, and four slim spoked wheels. Its bright blue paint and clean small shape make it look quick and friendly, and it zips through city streets and along country lanes. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
636. The Meteor is a sleek low gig with a long pointed front, a sloping rear deck, a small rear rail, silver rimmed spoked wheels, and a glossy dark paint finish with a bright streak along its side. Its narrow brass lamps and wide stance give it a racing look, and it streaks forward at very high speed pulled by a fast horse. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
637. The Blue Bigfoot is a tall blue freight wagon with a high covered bed, many large windows, a roof rack, a front ladder, and six heavy duty wheels for rough terrain. Its broad body and bright blue paint make it look like a rolling base camp, and it rumbles slowly and steadily across open land carrying a whole party. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
638. The Windwraith Mercury is a sleek silver carriage with a sharp wedge body, a mirrored silver finish, narrow slit windows, a low rear rail, and dark rimmed wheels. Its smooth panels and ghostly silver shine give it a swift phantom look, and it glides fast and silent along roads pulled by a pale horse. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
639. The Storm Fantasy is a metallic purple gig with a curved low body, a swirling purple and violet paint finish, a sculpted front, a rear rail, and dark five spoke wheels. Its storm cloud sheen and glowing purple trim give it a dreamlike racing look, and it surges forward with quick sharp handling pulled by a dark horse. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
640. The Galaxy Red Emperor is a grand luxury carriage in gold and black with a long polished body, gold trim, a tall black front panel, gold rimmed wheels, and a star flecked black roof. Its rich colors and stately shape give it an imperial look, and it rolls forward smooth and powerful like a parade carriage pulled by a black horse. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
641. The Red Gem Meteor is a bright red gig with a gemlike glossy red finish, a low sleek body, a clear rear rail, red rimmed wheels, and a sparkling ruby stripe down its center. Its jewel bright paint and sharp racing lines set it apart as a red gem version of a gig, and it darts forward fast with a flashing red gleam pulled by a red brown horse. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
642. The Windwraith Azure is a light blue carriage with a smooth curved body, a pale azure and white paint finish, a curved glass window, a thin rear rail, and silver wheels. Its cool sky blue shine and airy shape give it a light wind spirit look, and it flows fast along open roads with a soft blue glow pulled by a white horse. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
643. The Aurora Wings is a magenta and purple gig with wing shaped side panels, a wide rear rail, a glowing magenta stripe, a low front, and dark rimmed wheels. Its bright aurora colors shift from magenta to purple across its body, and it sweeps forward fast like a bird in flight pulled by a swift horse. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
644. The Thunderstorm is a storm gray carriage with electric blue lightning decals, a sharp angular body, a black glass window, a tall rear fin, and glowing blue wheel rims. Its dark storm paint and crackling blue lines give it a wild weather look, and it charges forward with sudden bursts of speed pulled by a gray horse. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
645. The Heart of Steel is a heavy steel plated armored carriage with riveted armor panels, a broad square frame, a glowing heart emblem on its side, a reinforced front guard, and thick armored wheels. Its dull gray steel and bold red heart mark give it a tough battle ready look, and it lumbers forward strong and steady through danger pulled by a heavy draft horse. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
646. The Sky Skiff is a small wooden sky boat with a curved hull, a pointed bow, a single white sail, brass trim, glowing engine pods at its stern, and small side wings for steering. Its light frame and bright sail give it a nimble scout ship look, and it floats and sails through the sky on wind and engine power. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
647. The Sky Galleon is a large flying ship with a long wooden hull, three tall masts with white and gold sails, a carved figurehead at its bow, side decks, brass lanterns, and glowing propellers at its stern. Its grand sails and ornate carvings give it a royal airship look, and it sails slowly and majestically through the clouds. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
648. The Golden Strider is a tall rideable bird with bright golden feathers, a long graceful neck, a strong orange beak, long sturdy legs, and a flowing golden tail. Its shining plumage and proud stance give it a noble mount look, and it runs fast across plains with long smooth strides. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
649. The hobby horse is a fast red push bike with a streamlined wooden frame, narrow high pressure wheels, drop handlebars, a slim saddle, and a bright red and silver paint finish. Its lean shape and thin wheels give it a pure speed look, and it races up muddy slopes and across cracked floors at top speed pushed by swift feet. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
650. The armored carriage is a sturdy war carriage with a compact steel frame, thick armor plates, a reinforced front guard, strong spoked wheels, and a bright steel and red paint finish. Its small tough shape is built for battle escort, and it rolls through danger with steady armor and careful control. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
651. The boat is a small wooden rowboat with a curved hull, plank seats, a pair of oars, a coiled rope, and a painted blue stripe along its side. Its simple open shape and weathered wood give it a humble harbor look, and it glides across calm water with slow steady strokes. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
652. The kayak is a narrow single person kayak with a pointed bow, a smooth green and yellow hull, a small cockpit, a double ended paddle, and a waterproof storage hatch. Its slim shape and bright colors give it a quick river craft look, and it cuts through water with fast smooth paddle strokes. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
653. The wagon plus horse is a wooden farm wagon with four large spoked wheels, plank sides, a canvas cover, hay bales in its bed, and a strong brown horse in harness pulling it from the front. The horse wears a leather harness with brass bells, and the wagon rolls slowly along dirt roads with a creaking steady pace. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
654. The roller skates are a pair of sturdy skates with high blue boots, white laces, silver buckles, a padded ankle collar, and four wooden wheels in a straight line under each boot. Their sporty colors and sleek wheel frames give them a fast street look, and they roll smooth and quick along paved paths with a gliding stride. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
655. The kick scooter is a flat wooden scooter with a curved deck, a tall handlebar, a bright graphic on its underside, black grip tape on top, and two small hard wheels on a wooden frame. Its simple shape and bold graphic give it a playful street look, and it rolls fast across smooth ground and hops over small bumps. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
656. The Embermane Charger is a strong chestnut riding horse with a bright ember red mane and tail, a dark leather saddle with brass fittings, and a red saddle blanket trimmed in gold. Its coat shines in the sun, its hooves strike the road with a steady rhythm, and it gallops hard across open plains with long smooth strides. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

## Section 10: Guides (3)

657. A mystical open book floating at chest height, its pages glowing with soft golden light and covered in shifting runes and handwritten notes, bound in deep purple leather with silver corner clasps and a small crystal set into the cover. The book is an AI assisted in-game wiki and contact book, the in-game device that holds answers to any question about the game and the names of Mankind met along the journey so they can be contacted again for a rematch. It looks like a mystical conversation, but it is simply a generic AI chat loaded with answers about the game, nothing more than the wiki and the contacts. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
658. A small glowing mystical orb hovering in the air, a sphere of pale blue and violet light with faint stars and swirling mist turning slowly inside it, ringed by thin silver bands etched with tiny runes. The orb is the guide given to the Mankind trainer who chooses magic, and it does the same job as the Grimoire, serving as the in-game wiki and contact book with answers to any question about the game and the names of Mankind met so they can be contacted again for a rematch. It looks like a mystical conversation, but it is simply a generic AI chat loaded with answers about the game, nothing more than the wiki and the contacts. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
659. A tiny bright fairy guide with delicate glowing wings and a soft golden light trailing behind her as she hovers in the air, wearing a simple pale green dress with small leaves and flowers woven into it. The fairy is the guide given to the Mankind trainer who chooses the Sword and attack, and she does the same job as the Grimoire, serving as the in-game wiki and contact book with answers to any question about the game and the names of Mankind met so they can be contacted again for a rematch. She looks like a mystical conversation, but she is simply a generic AI chat loaded with answers about the game, nothing more than the wiki and the contacts. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

## Section 11: UI (16)

660. FED-13 title screen with the gold game title centered over a dark painted sky and a distant mountain silhouette, a small glowing emblem below the title, and a plain press start line at the bottom. The layout is wide and calm with empty space around the title so the lettering stays the focus. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
661. Main menu screen with a deep blue Final Fantasy style menu window on the left holding the menu choices for starting, continuing, loading, and settings, and the gold FED-13 title at the top. A soft painted landscape fills the background behind the window with muted hills and sky. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
662. Dialogue box with portrait showing a blue Final Fantasy style text window across the bottom of the screen and a square Mankind portrait framed in gold at the left end of the box. The portrait shows a painted face in profile view with clear eyes and hair, and two short lines of plain dialogue text fill the rest of the window. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
663. Cutscene frame with dark letterbox bars at the top and bottom of a wide painted scene of a quiet town square at dusk, with small figures standing apart in the middle distance. A small blue dialogue window sits at the bottom inside the lower bar with one short line of text. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
664. Codex page with a parchment panel listing the five core Bondkin bloodlines in order, each entry showing a small painted emblem, a codex number, and two lines of plain description text. A gold header at the top reads Codex and a thin border frames the whole page. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
665. Realm Map screen showing a painted world map with winding routes, dungeon markers, and connection lines between five visible towns, with forests, mountains, and coast drawn in soft watercolor. A small gold compass sits in one corner and a plain legend box lists the eight location types. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
666. Puffkin shop screen with a small Puffkin shopkeeper standing behind a wooden counter on the left, and a blue Final Fantasy style menu window on the right listing shop goods in plain rows. Shelves with jars, folded cloth, and small crates fill the painted background behind the counter. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
667. Synthesis screen with a central workbench panel showing two collected material slots on the left and one finished item slot on the right, joined by a gold arrow. A blue menu window below lists the collected materials in plain rows with small painted icons. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
668. Save point screen with a glowing crystal marker on a stone base standing in a quiet painted clearing, with soft light pooling on the ground around it. A small blue menu window floats beside the marker offering the choice to save and rest. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
669. Judge card with a tall portrait card framed in gold, showing a stern robed Judge figure in the center panel above a plain text box listing Judge points. A small yellow card marker and a small red card marker sit in opposite corners of the card. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
670. Law card with a tall parchment card framed in gold, showing a scales emblem at the top and a short list of tournament laws in plain text below it. The card notes that laws apply in tournaments only, with a small Judge seal at the bottom. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
671. Anti-Law card with a tall dark parchment card framed in deep red, showing a crossed scales emblem at the top and a short list of forbidden tournament actions in plain text below it. A small warning seal sits at the bottom to set it apart from the Law card. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
672. Clan screen with a blue Final Fantasy style roster window listing clan members in plain rows, each row showing a small portrait, a clan title, and a level. A side panel shows clan turf, clan level, and the deploy limit of six members. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
673. Dispatch screen with a guild notice board panel listing dispatch missions in plain rows, each row showing a mission length of five, ten, or twenty days and a small reward icon. A side window shows the deployed party of six and the return date for each mission. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
674. Item icon set shown as one neat grid of small square painted icons on a plain dark background, covering healing items, materials, Mankind items, Bondkin items, and Beast items in separate rows. Each icon is a simple readable object such as a vial, a crystal, a pouch, a tool, or a scroll, drawn at the same size with a thin gold frame. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
675. Status icons set shown as one neat grid of small round painted icons on a plain dark background, covering battle statuses such as Break, Unbalanced, poison, sleep, burn, and paralysis. Each icon is a simple readable symbol such as a cracked shield, a tilted scale, a bubble, a moon, a flame, or a spark, drawn at the same size with a thin colored ring. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

## Section 12: Item Icons (14)

676. A matching icon set for the Mankind gathering tools and homestead workstations: a woodcutting axe, a mining pickaxe, a fishing rod, a rolled blueprint, and small workbench, construction workbench, raw material workbench, deconstruction bench, equipment repair table, mechanic station, homestead sign, storage cabinet, and repair parts icons. Every icon reads at a glance as a tool or station the trainer uses, never as a Bondkin or Beast item, with warm wood, iron, and canvas tones and clear silhouettes. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
677. A matching icon set for the Mankind class gear: the Soulblade as the FED-13 original great blade with a crossguard and crystal core, the opening choice Sword, Shield, and Staff, and blade, saber, dagger, katana, spear, axe, bow, gun, and fists weapon icons alongside armor, shoes, gloves, accessories, and costume icons. Each icon is a clean trainer gear piece in polished steel, leather, and cloth, with the Soulblade standing out as the hero weapon of the set. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
678. A matching icon set for role gathering material: wood, stone, ore, iron ore, copper ore, plants, fish, cedar bark, ash branch, tattered animal skin, flaxseed, circuit boards, and generic metals. Each icon shows raw unworked material in a small bundle or chunk, tied to the Logger, Miner, Gatherer, Hunter, Chef, Angler, Scavenger, and Firearms Master roles, with earthy browns, greys, and greens. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
679. A matching icon set for Bondkin items: a glowing Growth Item, a Soul wisp for the Morpher, the Satyr Flute, and the Beastmaster bell, flute, and harp, plus a guardian Totem Spirit emblem. No Totem Spirit names are given in the item list, so the Totem icons use unnamed guardian emblems in gold and deep blue, while the instruments stay small, wooden, and clearly playable. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
680. A matching icon set for Beast items: a speckled Beast Egg in varied shells, since Beasts have no weapons or equipment and only Beast related items. The icons stay soft and organic, with warm shell tones and a small nest motif, and the set notes that no daycare item is named in the item list. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
681. A matching icon set for the Mankind only Lattice Shards: Art Shards, Stat Shards, Ailment Shards, Bell Shards, Special Shards, Sepith in seven elements, EP Charge vials, and the Moon Lens. Each shard is a faceted crystal in its element color, Earth, Water, Fire, Wind, Time, Space, and Mirage, with the Bell Shards as small bells and the EP Charge icons as glowing vials, all clearly Mankind quartz and never Bondkin gear. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
682. A matching icon set for the three verified Mankind Core Shards, Skuld of Earth, Sirius of Wind, and Regulus of Fire, each a large central Lattice crystal with its element color and a distinct carved emblem. The Core Shard is Mankind only, it levels by battle experience and grants large stat boosts and special abilities, and it does not determine Bondkin affinity. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
683. A matching icon set for U-Material, a single key material shown as a radiant prismatic crystal fragment in shifting colors, in single pieces and in small counted stacks. U-Material is used only for Mankind Shard upgrades to Rare and Super Rare and for Mankind weapon upgrades, never for Bondkin gear. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
684. A matching icon set for vehicle parts: Material/G hull stock, Fuel, Steel, Screw, Recycled Material, and Silver, plus small Sky Skiff frame and panel pieces. Each icon is a sturdy mechanical part in gunmetal, brass, and oilcloth tones, built for crafting and repairing bikes and four wheeled vehicles at the Mechanic Station. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
685. A matching icon set for vehicle appearance parts, with the category name not decided and no named items in the item list, so the set shows generic paint, trim, decal, and finish swatches in bright racing colors as placeholders for the look only. Every icon reads as a cosmetic change to a vehicle body, never as a mechanical part or a repair item. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
686. A matching icon set for tuning parts, with the category name not decided and no named items in the item list, so the set shows generic dials, gauges, gears, and adjustment tools as placeholders for performance tuning only. Every icon reads as a fine adjustment to a vehicle, never as a repair part or an appearance part. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
687. A matching icon set for the three time stones, the Moon Stone, the Sun Stone, and the Dusk Stone, each a smooth round stone glowing with pale moonlight, bright sunlight, or deep dusk violet. The stones are tied to time based evolution, and which stone maps to which time is not decided in the item list. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
688. A matching icon set for breeding items: Beast Eggs in varied shells, feed bundles, and daycare care supplies, centered on the egg as the bred and hatched item. The icons stay warm and pastoral, with soft straw, shell, and leaf tones, and Beasts breed for eggs while Bondkin have full children instead. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
689. A matching icon set for fusion items, with the category name not decided and no named items in the item list, so the set shows generic paired and merging emblem tokens as placeholders for the fusion idea only. Every icon reads as two things becoming one, never as a breeding item or a shard, and the fusion routes of breeding, daycare, and breeding items use no named fusion item yet. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

## Section 15: Town Art (60)

764. Vexhall, a sealed black stone citadel town with high walls, torchlit streets, and masked stewards in dark grey coats holding secret meetings behind locked doors. Grey coated couriers carry locked orders past tall black towers while banners of The Thirteen hang from every gate. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
765. Umbragate, a dark gate town beneath a great black arch, with grey coated guards checking sealed passes and torchlight burning in iron braziers along the shadowed wall. Horse drawn carts wait in line at the gate while tall black towers rise behind the gloom. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
766. Hollowmoot, a hollow hall town where masked delegates gather in silence around a great echoing moot hall ringed by empty benches and locked side doors. Pale lantern light falls across the stone floor while hooded figures in grey coats stand along the walls. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
767. Nightcourt, a night trial town of black stone courts and shuttered windows, with masked judges hearing sealed cases by lantern light in a high roofed hall. Narrow streets wind between dark towers while a single bell hangs silent above the court square. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
768. Shroudmark, a veiled market town where traders in grey coats and sealed masks sell locked boxes and coded ledgers from stalls draped in dark cloth. Shrouds hang over every counter while lantern light glows behind the curtains and sealed letters change hands in the square. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
769. Whitehollow, a pale white stone town of bright empty rooms and secret doors, where pale robed keepers chalk new marks on walls that change shape overnight. White towers and pale arches gleam in cold light while a long white hall bends away into the distance. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
770. Doorwatch, a town of door watchers guarding a hundred locked doors along one long white hall, each watcher holding a ring of iron keys and a lantern that never goes out. Tall pale doors line both sides of the corridor while white stone steps climb toward a sealed gate at the far end. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
771. Vellum, a pale parchment town of scribes and map makers, with ink pots, drying racks, and scroll shelves filling every workshop along a quiet white lane. Thin vellum sheets covered in maps of the shifting halls hang from lines above the street while scribes work at long pale tables. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
772. Corridale, a town built along a single long corridor that bends and narrows without warning, its pale doors opening onto rooms that were not there the day before. Lanterns hang at uneven heights along the winding hall while white plaster walls curve away into shadow. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
773. Lostwick, a winding lane town of crooked streets and small blue lanterns, where the only safe road home is marked by pale light through a thin drifting fog. Crooked timber houses lean over the lanes while pale robed guides lead travelers between the blue lamps. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
774. Cipherest, a town of code makers and spymasters where cipher wheels turn in every workshop and sealed letters change hands in quiet courtyards. Hooded figures in dark cloaks trade small locked boxes beneath tall grey towers while coded papers flutter from lines above the street. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
775. Whisperhold, a hushed town of quiet halls and listening posts, where hooded listeners in dark cloaks write down every soft word spoken in the square. Low stone houses crowd around a silent courtyard while pale lanterns burn behind shuttered windows. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
776. Inkwatch, a town of ink makers and message watchers where black ink, sealed scrolls, and carrier pigeons fill the workshops along a narrow lane. Pigeon lofts crown the rooftops while scribes seal letters with dark wax at long tables in the courtyard. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
777. Veilmarket, a veiled market town where masked sellers trade in coded prices and wrapped goods behind curtains of dark cloth. Veils hang over every stall while buyers pay with sealed tokens and lantern light glows through the layered fabric. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
778. Shadowpost, a hidden post town of couriers and dead drops, where sealed satchels pass from hand to hand in shadowed lanes beneath low dark roofs. Hooded riders lead horses through the gloom while a lone lantern marks the post house door. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
779. Portalwatch, a town guarding a great shimmering portal, where portal wardens in bright robes check every traveler and wizard towers ring the glowing gate. Light spills from the portal across the cobbled square while banners and braziers line the road to the gate. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
780. Spellmere, a lakeside town where spells ripple across still water, its wizard towers, floating lights, and spell shops lining a shore that glows after dark. Lanterns drift above the lake while robed wizards walk the waterfront beneath tall pointed towers. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
781. Runestead, a town of carved runes and standing stones, where rune smiths cut glowing marks into rock and every doorway carries a ward against stray magic. Tall rune stones ring the square while sparks of light trace the carved lines and smoke rises from the smiths forge. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
782. Wandwick, a town of wand makers where carved wood, polished cores, and wand shops fill the lanes, and apprentices test new wands in a fenced yard behind the guild hall. Stacks of pale timber and bundles of carved wands lean against every shop front while bright sparks drift over the yard. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
783. Arcanmere, a deep magic town on a misty lake where arcane libraries, floating lanterns, and hooded scholars keep the oldest spells. The water shimmers with stored light beneath tall dark towers while mist curls around the library steps. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
784. Joyford, a bright cheerful town of festivals, music, and sunny lanes, where laughter spills from every doorway and flower garlands hang across the streets. Musicians play in the square while bright banners flutter above market stalls piled with fruit and ribbons. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
785. Sorrowsong, a mournful town of grey rain and weeping willows, where singers in dark cloaks sing slow songs of loss and every window keeps a candle for the missing. Rain falls on the cobbled lanes while willow branches trail into the slow river and pale candles glow behind the glass. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
786. Emberhold, a town of burning hearths and forge fires where ember lit streets glow red at dusk and smiths work through the night. Sparks rise from the forge chimneys while warm light spills from every doorway onto the dark cobbles. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
787. Dreadhollow, a fearful town of dark fog and shuttered windows, where the streets whisper, the lamps burn low, and few folk walk out after the evening bell. Fog drifts between the dark houses while a single pale lamp glows over the empty square. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
788. Stillmere, a calm town of still water and quiet halls, where the lake never ripples, the gardens never rustle, and every visitor speaks softly. Pale stone houses line the mirror smooth shore while white flowers float on the water beneath a quiet sky. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
789. Thornwick, a thorn walled town where rose gates guard a pale palace and thorn hedges ring every garden, seat of the queen regent. Red roses climb the pale walls while guards in green and gold stand at the thorn gate and banners fly from the palace towers. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
790. Appleford, an orchard town of red apple trees, cider presses, and market stalls, where the sweet groves crowd the lanes and the fenced poison apple orchards stand locked beyond. Baskets of red apples fill the square while the cider press turns beside a timber barn and blossom drifts over the roofs. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
791. Mirrorhold, a town of mirror halls and polished glass, where every hall reflects its visitors twice over and mirror makers polish great silvered panels in sunlit workshops. Tall mirrors line the square while light bounces between the glass walls and pale towers. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
792. Forestgate, a gate town at the forest edge with timber walls, woodcutters, and hunters, where the road into the deep woods starts beneath a carved arch of oak. Stacks of cut timber line the lanes while the dark green forest rises behind the gate and smoke curls from the woodcutters cottages. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
793. Grimmere, a dark fairy tale town of crooked houses and grim lanes, where old tales are told by firelight and every lane ends at a door that should stay shut. Crooked timber roofs lean over the cobbles while candles glow in small windows and a dark wood presses close behind the town. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
794. Copperton, a copper roofed town at the mountain foot where copper smiths hammer brass pipes and kettles, and green copper domes catch the sun above stone workshops. Smoke rises from the smithy chimneys while copper pots, pipes, and kettles crowd the market stalls. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
795. Gearford, a town of great turning gears and workshops, where clockwork lifts, brass bridges, and engine halls fill the valley beneath a steep mountain wall. Huge brass gears turn slowly above the rooftops while engineers in leather aprons tend the machines in the square. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
796. Steamwick, a steam vent town of hissing pipes, bath houses, and engine halls, where white steam curls from every grate and brass gauges line the workshop walls. Steam drifts over the cobbled streets while bath house roofs and brass chimneys rise through the pale cloud. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
797. Wirehold, a town of wire and cable where signal towers, brass instruments, and coiled wire fill the yards, and messengers send tapped signals from tower to tower. Tall brass towers rise above the rooftops while coils of copper wire and brass dials crowd every workshop yard. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
798. Clockmere, a lakeside town of clocks and timekeepers, where clock towers ring the still water, brass clock faces mark every square, and the great town clock never stops. Clock hands gleam on every tower while the calm lake reflects the brass faces and pale stone houses. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
799. Sunmane, a sun baked town of golden roofs and clan banners, where the streets glow in the afternoon heat and clan elders meet beneath a great shaded awning. Golden banners snap in the dry wind while thatch and clay houses line the dusty square. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
800. Tallgrass, a grassland town of tall grass and thatch roofs, where hunters, herders, and grass cutters work the open plain and the wind bends the fields in waves. Tall golden grass sways around the low thatch houses while a dirt track winds out across the plain. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
801. Goldmane, a golden banner town where the great clan hall stands at the center and strength trials are held in the dusty yard. Gold banners hang from the tall timber hall while warriors train in the open yard and the savanna stretches away under a wide sky. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
802. Kingwatch, a watch town guarding the king's road, where tall watch towers, trial grounds, and training yards line the route and guards drill from dawn to dusk. Banners fly from the watch towers while guards march the dusty road and the trial grounds lie open beside the gate. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
803. Dustplain, a dusty plain town of dry wells, caravan rests, and open markets, where traders water their horses at the stone troughs and dust coats every roof. Caravans rest in the open yard while market awnings shade baskets of grain and the plain rolls away to the horizon. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
804. Coralport, a coral harbor town where coral halls rise from the reef, shell boats crowd the docks, and pearl lanterns light the water. Pink and orange coral arches frame the harbor while shell boats rock at the piers beneath a bright sea sky. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
805. Tidehollow, a hollow reef town of tide pools and echoing caves, where the tide fills the lower streets twice a day and shell collectors work the pools at low water. Clear pools gleam between the coral rocks while cave mouths open in the cliff and shells pile in baskets along the shore. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
806. Pearlwick, a pearl market town of white stalls and pearl divers, where divers bring up gleaming shells, pearl cutters work at open benches, and buyers haggle over strands of white pearl. White awnings shade the pearl stalls while the calm blue water laps the docks behind the market. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
807. Deepmere, a deep trench town of dark blue halls and glowing lanterns, where the water turns cold and still, and deep divers in brass helms walk the trench floor. Blue light glows from lantern posts along the trench walls while dark halls sink into the deep below. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
808. Shellford, a shell paved town where shell houses line a ford across the reef shallows, and carts cross the low water at ebb tide beneath strings of hanging shells. White shells pave the streets while the shallow water sparkles around the ford and shell garlands hang from every doorway. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
809. Wavecrest, a wave beaten cliff town where a tall lookout watches the sea and voyaging canoes rest on the shingle below. White spray breaks against the cliffs while banners fly from the lookout tower and the open ocean stretches to the horizon. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
810. Canoebay, a bay town of canoes and paddlers, where fish drying racks line the sand, net makers mend sails, and every household keeps a canoe by its door. Painted canoes crowd the shore while nets dry on wooden frames and smoke rises from the fish smokehouses. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
811. Starpath, a town of star readers and wayfinders, where star charts cover the workshop walls, a night sky observatory crowns the hill, and sailors learn the star paths by heart. The observatory dome gleams above the town while star charts hang from the market stalls and the evening sky fills with early stars. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
812. Tidereach, a tidal town of long jetties and tide mills, where the mill wheels turn with the rising water and boats rise and fall against the wooden piers. Long wooden jetties reach into the bay while tide mill wheels turn slowly and gulls circle over the moored boats. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
813. Palmford, a palm shaded town at a lagoon ford, where fruit stalls, woven mats, and resting voyagers crowd the shore, and the crossing is marked by carved palm posts. Palm fronds shade the sandy lanes while the calm lagoon sparkles beside the ford and baskets of fruit fill the stalls. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
814. Pawford, a cobblestone city town where constables walk the main square, horse drawn carts fill the lanes, and timber and brick houses crowd the market. The city hall rises above the square while cart wheels rattle over the cobbles and shop signs swing in the breeze. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
815. Burrowgate, a low gate district of brick houses and burrow lanes, where cart drivers, porters, and night watchers live close to the city gate and the streets stay busy till late. Lanterns glow over the low brick lanes while carts roll through the great gate and laundry lines cross between the houses. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
816. Whiskerwick, a market district of food stalls, bakeries, and shop signs, where the smell of fresh bread fills the lanes and traders call out their goods from dawn. Painted shop signs swing above the stalls while baskets of bread, cheese, and fruit crowd the cobbled market square. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
817. Talltree, a tall timber district of tree lined streets and high houses, where the tallest homes in the city rise above the lanes and laundry lines cross between the upper windows. Tall trees shade the steep lanes while timber houses climb three and four storeys high above the street. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
818. Denholme, a quiet home district of gardens, hearths, and low houses, where the city rests after dark and every lane ends at a small green with a well. Smoke curls from low chimneys while flower gardens fill the front yards and a stone well stands on the quiet green. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
819. Gravewick, a graveyard town where leaning stones, iron gates, and a cold chapel stand under a grey sky, and the final Trial Battle is held. Mist drifts between the gravestones while a tall dark chapel rises at the top of the hill and crows circle the iron gate. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
820. Mistmoor, a mist covered moor town where fog rolls across the lanes, lantern posts mark the road, and ghost hunters wait out the night in the shuttered inn. Pale fog swallows the low stone houses while lantern light glows faintly through the mist along the empty road. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
821. Hollowbell, a bell tower town where hollow bells ring without a hand to pull them, its echoing streets lined with dark houses and a great cracked bell above the square. The tall cracked bell tower rises over the town while dark rooftops crowd the square below and pale light falls through the tower arch. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
822. Wraithford, a pale town of wraiths and cold light, where grey mist hangs over the ford, the houses stand white and still, and the river runs slow beneath a stone bridge. White houses gleam through the mist while the slow river slides under the bridge and pale lanterns burn at the ford. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
823. Lanternfall, a lantern lit town where falling lantern light fills the streets, ghost hunters hang fresh lanterns at every door, and the old mansion waits at the top of the hill. Warm lanterns glow from every doorway while the dark mansion looms above the town and the hill road climbs through the light. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.

## Section 16: Mankind Class Sprites (52)

824. Warrior, a Mankind Warrior in a balanced steel plate outfit with a broad sword and a round shield, standing in a steady guard stance. Show the front view, back view, left side view, right side view, plus battle front view and battle back view with the same colors, outfit, and weapon in every view. Required views: front view, back view, left side view, right side view, plus battle front view and battle back view. Battle sprites: the battle front view and battle back view are the Pokemon style 1v1 battle sprites, side view, larger scale, the subject facing its opponent. The front view, back view, left side view, and right side view are the FFTA grid battle unit sprites, smaller scale, isometric grid units. ## Section 18: Named Vehicles (39)

890. The Snowdrift Palfrey is a gentle white riding horse with a pale silver mane and tail, a soft blue saddle blanket, a light leather saddle with silver fittings, and neat shod hooves. Its calm eyes and smooth gait make it a trusted road companion, and it trots steady through snow dusted lanes and quiet town streets. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
891. The Copperhoof Runner is a lean copper brown riding horse with a dark mane, a plain brown leather saddle, copper studded bridle straps, and bright polished copper horseshoes. Its slim build and quick feet give it a fast courier look, and it runs swift along dirt roads carrying messages and small parcels. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
892. The Mistvale Courser is a tall gray riding horse with a mist pale coat, a long silver mane, a deep green saddle blanket, and a leather saddle with brass buckles. Its quiet step and keen eyes suit foggy valley roads, and it moves smooth and sure through morning mist and damp forest trails. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
893. The Thornfield Destrier is a heavy black riding horse with a thick dark mane, a broad chest, a red and black saddle blanket, and a sturdy war saddle with iron fittings. Its powerful build and steady glare give it a knightly escort look, and it marches strong and unhurried along guarded roads and castle lanes. Style: Level 3 HD 2D, 256x256 sprite style, FFTA2 DS look, hand painted Ivalice watercolor.
